**Rolling window:** 2026-11-16 through 2026-11-22

## Best date plans

### 2026-11-21 — Barnard/Columbia Dances at New York Live Arts — Saturday Matinee + Iara

**Barnard/Columbia Dances at New York Live Arts — Saturday Matinee, then a meal.** Brazilian and Amazonian at Iara. It is 1.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 2 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=219+West+19th+Street&destination=205+Allen+Street&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://newyorklivearts.org/event/barnard-columbia-dances-at-new-york-live-arts-5/) · [Event booking](https://newyorklivearts.my.salesforce-sites.com/ticket/#/events/a0SVt00000gDW57MAG) · [Restaurant](https://iara.nyc/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/iara)

### 2026-11-17 — Lightscape Lodge at Yellow Magnolia Cafe + Heel Toe Rodeo with Juliet Hartz

**Dinner, then Heel Toe Rodeo with Juliet Hartz.** Seasonal American prix fixe at Lightscape Lodge at Yellow Magnolia Cafe. It is 1.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Doors 7 pm — eat first.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=990+Washington+Avenue%2C+inside+Brooklyn+Botanic+Garden+at+Lily+Pool+Terrace&destination=635+Sackett+Street&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://littlefieldnyc.com/event/?wfea_eb_id=2003414056373) · [Event booking](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/heel-toe-rodeo-tickets-2003414056373) · [Restaurant](https://www.bbg.org/feature/lightscape/lightscape_lodge_dinners) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/yellow-magnolia-cafe?date=2026-11-20&seats=2)

### 2026-11-16 — SKATE at Domino Park + Iara

**SKATE at Domino Park, then a meal.** Brazilian and Amazonian at Iara. It is 1.3 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It runs to 9 pm, so this is a late stop afterwards.

### 2026-11-16 — Altbau Bakery + Bodega Cats of New York — Dan Rimada book launch

**Dinner, then Bodega Cats of New York — Dan Rimada book launch.** Bakery, sourdough bread and pastries at Altbau Bakery. It is 2.0 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Bodega Cats of New York — Dan Rimada book launch starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=184+North+8th+Street&destination=28+Adams+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11201&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://powerhousearena.com/events/book-launch-bodega-cats-of-new-york-by-dan-rimada/) · [Event booking](https://events.ticketleap.com/tickets/powerhousebookstores/book-launch-bodega-cats-of-new-york-by-dan-rimada) · [Restaurant](https://altbaubakery.com/)

### 2026-11-16 — So This Is Paris with live piano + Casa Oaxaca

**So This Is Paris with live piano, then dinner.** Mexican at Casa Oaxaca. It is 1.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 6:20 pm, so eat afterwards.

### 2026-11-16 — November Variations: Stuart Bogie and Buck McDaniel — Monday + Brasserie Boulud

**November Variations: Stuart Bogie and Buck McDaniel — Monday, then dinner.** French brasserie at Brasserie Boulud. It is 1.6 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 6 pm, so eat afterwards.

35mm Import. Made the year before Oshima’s In Realm of the Senses, Noboru Tanaka’s rendition of the Sada Abe incident rests with Junko Miyashita’s fierce portrayal of the infamous figure, whose all-consuming affair culminates in a notorious act that would grip a nation already in the throes of rising militarism.

A pinnacle of the art of filmic portraiture, Gregory Markopoulos’s GALAXIE comprises thirty short portraits of men and women from across the spectrum of the mid-1960s cultural scene in New York City. ## Where to eat

NYC dining picks for November 16, 2026: lunch deals, happy hours, neighborhood restaurants and upcoming Thanksgiving menus. Future events and recurring service restrictions are distinguished from options available today.

Dining research: 2026-11-16T10:41:17+00:00

No Michelin award claims have been added. GUI's own structured data describes it as Michelin Selected, not Michelin-starred: https://guinyc.com/gui-lunch-menu. Its linked Michelin listing returned no readable content: https://guide.michelin.com/us/en/new-york-state/new-york/restaurant/gui-1207502. Current Michelin distinctions were therefore not independently verified.

### GUI's $8 three-course lunch upgrade

Deals & happy hours — GUI Steakhouse

Add a lunch-sized Caesar or market salad and one scoop of ice cream or sorbet to an eligible main. The $19 mushroom-and-tofu pot rice makes the combination $27 before any unspecified charges.

Price: Three-course upgrade +$8; eligible mushroom-and-tofu pot rice $19; kimchi wagyu fried rice $21; prime galbi pot rice $23.  
Schedule: Daily lunch, noon–2:30pm.  
Conditions: Upgrade applies to the Prime Rib Dip and items under Lunch Favorites, not automatically to every main. An eligible main must be purchased. Reservation requirement, tax, tip and service charges are not stated. Holiday applicability is unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://guinyc.com/gui-lunch-menu)

### GUI's $65 three-course pre-theater menu runs until closing

Deals & happy hours — GUI Steakhouse

A fixed-price dinner with one appetizer, main and dessert; main choices include hanger steak frites, chicken, branzino and spicy rigatoni.

Price: $65 per person for three courses.  
Schedule: Daily, 4pm to close; posted dinner closing times are 10pm Monday–Saturday and 9pm Sunday.  
Conditions: Choose one item from each course. Despite the pre-theater label, the menu states availability until closing. Reservation requirement, minimum party size, tax, tip and service charges are not stated. Thanksgiving applicability is unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://guinyc.com/gui-pre-theater-menu)

### Junoon's $43/$49 lunch prix fixe, Friday–Sunday

Deals & happy hours — Junoon

The Prestige lunch offers two or three courses with basmati rice and naan, including vegetarian options alongside chicken, shrimp and goat dishes.

Price: Two courses $43; three courses $49.  
Schedule: Friday–Sunday, 11:45am–2:30pm. No Monday–Thursday lunch is listed.  
Conditions: 20% service charge for parties of five or more. Reservations recommended, especially weekends. Official policy welcomes children above age 10 and requests smart-casual dress. Tax, other gratuity treatment, holiday exceptions and additional minimums are not stated.  
[Source](https://www.junoonnyc.com/menus/)

### The Fulton's $10 half-dozen oyster hour

A weekday waterfront oyster stop with a clearly priced half-dozen rather than an unspecified happy-hour discount.

Price: Six oysters $10.  
Schedule: Monday–Friday, 3:30–5pm.  
Conditions: Maximum 12 oysters per guest. A drink purchase requirement, seating restriction, reservation requirement, tax, tip and service charges are not stated. Other listed food is not necessarily discounted. Holiday applicability is unconfirmed.

2026-11-25 · 7:30 PM · theater · Regular seats $70–$98 plus $7 handling, totaling $77–$105; premium seats cost more. · Polonsky Shakespeare Center · Fort Greene · Brooklyn · Score 69.0  
Daniel Fish directs Sophokles's tragedy in Anne Carson's translation, with choral music by Ted Hearne. The production explores grief, revenge, and the consequences of familial violence.
**Timing:** November 25 performance at 7:30pm; additional performances continue through December 6.
**Practical note:** No late seating. November 25 is listed without a sold-out label, but individual seats were not inspected. Regular tickets remain within the $120 budget before any additional unlisted charges.
**Why now:** Multiple performances beyond the seven-day window are already sold out, reducing choices before the confirmed closing.
**Hot booking alert:** The official calendar confirms a December 6 closing, marks November 24 and December 1–3 sold out, and labels December 4–5 and the final evening limited to no availability.

through 2027-04-02 · New York School of Interior Design Gallery · Upper East Side · Manhattan · Free; confirm registration  

### [You Should Be Dancing: New York, 1976 and Beyond](https://www.nyhistory.org/exhibitions/you-should-be-dancing-new-york-1976-and-beyond)

through 2027-04-04 · The New York Historical · Upper West Side · Manhattan · From $24 per listing; check conditions  

### [Roy Lichtenstein: Like New](https://whitney.org/exhibitions/lichtenstein)

2026-10-11 through 2027-05-10 · Whitney Museum of American Art · Meatpacking District · Manhattan · $30  
A major retrospective spans Lichtenstein's paintings, sculpture, prints, film and recreated murals.

### [Roy Lichtenstein: 1997](https://whitney.org/exhibitions/lichtenstein-1997)

through 2027-06-01 · Whitney Museum of American Art · Meatpacking District · Manhattan · Check current price  

### [New Exhibitions at Brooklyn Museum](https://brooklynmuseum.org/exhibitions/upcoming)

through 2027-06-27 · Brooklyn Museum · Prospect Heights · Brooklyn · From $20 per listing; check conditions  

### [Stephanie Dinkins: Anchoring Home](https://www.statenislandmuseum.org/exhibitions/stephanie-dinkins-anchoring-home/)

open since 2026-11-14 · Staten Island Museum · Snug Harbor · Staten Island · $8 suggested adult admission; $5 students/seniors  
An evolving installation combines oral histories, video, sound and AI-generated imagery. Retrofitted telephones invite visitors to contribute stories.

- **[Whitney Museum free Friday evening](https://whitney.org/visit)** — Fridays · Whitney Museum of American Art · Meatpacking District
